&+\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{k}\varepsilon{}^{j i}W{}_{\beta \alpha}\mathcal R(S){}^{a \beta k \gamma l}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\delta}\lambda{}_{\delta l} -\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}^{i j}W{}^{\alpha}{}_{\alpha}X{}_{k}{}^{k}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}W\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{64}W^{-2}\lambda{}_{\alpha i}X{}^{\alpha i}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}W\big) +\frac{1}{64}W^{-2}\lambda{}_{\alpha i}X{}^{\alpha i}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}W\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-2}\lambda{}_{\alpha i}X{}_{j}{}^{j}\big(\nabla^{a}\mathcal R(\mathbb D){}_{a}{}^{\alpha i}\big) -\frac{1}{48}W^{-2}\lambda{}_{\alpha i}X{}_{j}{}^{j}\mathcal T{}^{a \alpha \beta}\mathcal R(\mathbb D){}_{a \beta}{}^{i}\\
&-\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}_{\alpha i}X{}_{j}{}^{j}\mathcal R(K){}_{a}{}^{\alpha i a} -\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}\varepsilon{}^{i j}W{}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}_{\alpha \beta}\big(\nabla_{a}W\big)\big(\nabla^{b}\nabla_{b}W\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{k}\varepsilon{}^{j i}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}_{\beta \alpha}\mathcal R(\mathbb D){}_{a}{}^{\beta k}\big(\nabla^{b}\nabla_{b}W\big) -\frac{1}{48}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{k}\varepsilon{}^{j i}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}_{\beta \alpha}\big(\nabla_{a}W\big)\big(\nabla^{b}\mathcal R(\mathbb D){}_{b}{}^{\beta k}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{k}\varepsilon{}^{j i}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}_{\beta \alpha}\big(\nabla_{a}W\big)\mathcal T{}^{b \beta \gamma}\mathcal R(\mathbb D){}_{b \gamma}{}^{k} -\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{k}\varepsilon{}^{j i}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}_{\beta \alpha}\big(\nabla_{a}W\big)\mathcal R(K){}_{b}{}^{\beta k b}\\
&-\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}^{i j}W{}^{\alpha \beta}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{k}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}\lambda{}_{\alpha k}\big) +\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\alpha}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{i}\big)\\
&+\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\beta}\big) +\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\big(\nabla^{a}\mathcal T{}_{a}{}^{\beta \gamma}\big)W{}_{\gamma \beta}\\
&+\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}_{a}{}^{\beta \gamma}\big(\nabla^{a}W{}_{\gamma \beta}\big) -\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}\big(\nabla^{a}\mathcal R(S){}_{a \beta}{}^{i \beta j}\big)\\
&+\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}\big(\nabla_{a}W{}_{\gamma \beta}\big) +\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}\mathcal T{}_{a \gamma}{}^{\delta}W{}_{\delta \beta}\\
&-\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}\mathcal T{}^{a}{}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\mathcal R(S){}_{a \gamma}{}^{i \beta j} +\frac{1}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{j}\mathcal R(S){}^{a \beta i \gamma}{}_{j}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\delta}W{}_{\delta \beta}\\
&+\frac{1}{96}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}\mathcal R(S){}^{a}{}_{\beta}{}^{i \gamma j}W{}_{a}{}^{b}(\Gamma{}_{b}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\beta} -\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}^{i j}W{}^{\alpha \beta}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{k}\big(\nabla^{a}\mathcal T{}_{a \alpha}{}^{\gamma}\big)\lambda{}_{\gamma k}\\
&+\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\alpha}\big(\nabla^{a}\mathcal T{}_{a \beta}{}^{\gamma}\big)\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{i} +\frac{i}{24}W^{-2}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\big(\nabla^{a}\mathcal T{}_{a \beta}{}^{\gamma}\big)W{}^{\beta}{}_{\gamma}
\end{aligned}
\tag{S4-200-352}
